Evidence gaps and comparison limits

Missing measurements, unknown gates, omitted runs, original support availability, actual runtime behavior, production effectiveness and portability limit interpretation. Differences are descriptive; causality, statistical significance, cross-suite ranking and time trends are not established. Static scans and configuration do not measure behavioral benefit.

No analyst reports or independent assessments supplied. Use the deterministic next steps.

Skills and coverage

Evaluation groups (arms) compare recorded outputs with and without skill. Coverage is not a quality rating.

Coverage in supplied evidence
SkillBehavioral evidenceConfigured coverageStatic evidence

How to read the measures

Evaluator-reported (native) metrics use the evaluator's original score units. Skill Lift is its with-minus-without difference. Grading criteria (rubric) and mandatory checks (gates) record separate outcomes. Effectiveness, Correctness and Discoverability are with-skill rubric dimensions; corresponding without-skill headline dimensions are not supplied by this public contract. Values are preserved, not recomputed from rounded case scores. Case deltas use descriptive arm means only where all planned attempts are scored. A positive delta is higher, a negative delta is lower; neither explains why. Mandatory gates are separate from rubric passes. Unknown counts, scores and gates stay unknown.

Seeded mutation detectionUnavailableUnavailableUnavailable

incomplete_required_population. Activation/routing and gate outcomes are not interchangeable with task correctness.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Fraction of configured seeded mutants rejected by recognized assertions or the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch after the identical submission passes the reference. Unrecognized test failures, discovery failures and infrastructure failures remain unmeasured.

Metric powershell-pester-6.mutation-detection.v2; competency mutation_detection; unit fraction. Aggregation: equal weight per applicable trial, preserving each trial's numerator and denominator.

incomplete_required_population. Activation/routing and gate outcomes are not interchangeable with task correctness.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Reference correctness and detection of every configured seeded mutant must both hold. This version accepts recognized assertions and the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch; it never credits reference failures or unclassified execution failures.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Fraction of configured seeded mutants rejected by recognized assertions or the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch after the identical submission passes the reference. Unrecognized test failures, discovery failures and infrastructure failures remain unmeasured.

Primary outcome: reference-correct work detecting all seeded defects. Skill access is recorded separately in the attempt ledger and does not demonstrate benefit. A tie, absent-arm advantage or inconclusive result is legitimate.

incomplete_required_population. Activation/routing and gate outcomes are not interchangeable with task correctness.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Fraction of configured seeded mutants rejected by recognized assertions or the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch after the identical submission passes the reference. Unrecognized test failures, discovery failures and infrastructure failures remain unmeasured.

Metric powershell-pester-6.mutation-detection.v2; competency mutation_detection; unit fraction. Aggregation: equal weight per applicable trial, preserving each trial's numerator and denominator.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Fraction of configured seeded mutants rejected by recognized assertions or the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch after the identical submission passes the reference. Unrecognized test failures, discovery failures and infrastructure failures remain unmeasured.

Seeded mutation detection

Measured values · powershell-pester-6.mutation-detection.v2
MetricWith skill meanWithout skill meanWith minus without
Seeded mutation detection+0.8000 (positive)+0.8000 (positive)0.0000 (zero)

descriptive_reported_measurement_only. Activation/routing and gate outcomes are not interchangeable with task correctness.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Fraction of configured seeded mutants rejected by recognized assertions or the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch after the identical submission passes the reference. Unrecognized test failures, discovery failures and infrastructure failures remain unmeasured.

Metric powershell-pester-6.mutation-detection.v2; competency mutation_detection; unit fraction. Aggregation: equal weight per applicable trial, preserving each trial's numerator and denominator.

Joint test quality0.0000 (zero)0.0000 (zero)0.0000 (zero)

descriptive_reported_measurement_only. Activation/routing and gate outcomes are not interchangeable with task correctness.

Metric definition, applicability and scoring basis

Reference correctness and detection of every configured seeded mutant must both hold. This version accepts recognized assertions and the pinned Pester dependency-parameter mismatch; it never credits reference failures or unclassified execution failures.

Metric powershell-pester-6.test-quality.v2; competency test_quality; unit binary_score. Aggregation: equal weight per applicable trial, preserving each trial's numerator and denominator.
